    var path = (window.location.pathname || '/').split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content')) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;

    var parent = item.parentElement;
    while (parent) {
      var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
      if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
      parent = parent.parentElement;
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}

pointer-events-none" id="background-color"></span><style data-custom-css-index="0" data-custom-css-path="style.css">/* Gerstner Programm - Primary Font */
@font-face {
  font-family: 'Gerstner Programm';
  src: url('https://assets.p0web.com/Gerstner-ProgrammRegular.woff2') format('woff2');
  font-weight: 400;
  font-style: normal;
  font-display: swap;
}

@font-face {
  font-family: 'Gerstner Programm';
  src: url('https://assets.p0web.com/Gerstner-ProgrammMedium.woff2') format('woff2');
  font-weight: 500;
  font-style: normal;
  font-display: swap;
}

/* Apply font family globally with fallbacks */
body, html {
  font-family: 'Gerstner Programm', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Oxygen, Ubuntu, Cantarell, sans-serif;
}

/* FT System Mono - Secondary Font (Code) */
@font-face {
  font-family: 'FT System Mono';
  src: url('https://assets.p0web.com/FTSystemMono-Regular.woff2') format('woff2');
  font-weight: 400;
  font-style: normal;
  font-display: swap;
}

@font-face {
  font-family: 'FT System Mono';
  src: url('https://assets.p0web.com/FTSystemMono-Medium.woff2') format('woff2');
  font-weight: 500;
  font-style: normal;
  font-display: swap;
}

/* Apply FT System Mono to code elements */
code, pre, .code, [class*="language-"], kbd, samp, tt {
  font-family: 'FT System Mono', monospace !important;
}

/* Global heading font weights - make them less bold */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {
  font-weight: 500 !important; /* medium weight instead of bold/semibold */
}

/* Remove highlighting/backgrounds from card titles and headings */
h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Remove any mark/highlight elements from headings */
h1 mark, h2 mark, h3 mark, h4 mark, h5 mark, h6 mark {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
  color: inherit !important;
}

/* For extra light headings where explicitly needed */
.font-normal h1,
.font-normal h2,
.font-normal h3 {
  font-weight: 400 !important;
}

/* Consistent border radius for code snippets and cards */
pre,
code,
[id*="code-group"],
.not-prose,
[class*="codeblock"],
[role="tabpanel"],
.mint-card,
[class*="Card"],
.rounded-xl,
.rounded-lg,
.rounded-2xl {
  border-radius: 0.375rem !important; /* 5px equivalent to rounded-md */
}

/* Specific targeting for Mintlify code blocks */
.flex.flex-col.not-prose.relative.overflow-hidden {
  border-radius: 0.375rem !important;
}

/* Override blue colors with Parallel orange */
/* Active tab in code blocks */
[role="tab"][aria-selected="true"],
[role="tab"][data-headlessui-state="selected"] {
  color: #FB631B !important;
  border-color: #FB631B !important;
}

/* Tab border bottom */
[role="tab"][aria-selected="true"] .absolute,
[role="tab"][data-headlessui-state="selected"] .absolute {
  border-color: #FB631B !important;
}

/* Remove any blue backgrounds in dark mode */
.dark .codeblock-dark,
.dark [class*="codeblock"],
.dark pre {
  background-color: #1d1b16 !important;
}

/* Ensure code block backgrounds match theme */
[class*="codeblock"],
.not-prose {
  background-color: #fcfcfa !important;
}

.dark [class*="codeblock"],
.dark .not-prose {
  background-color: #1d1b16 !important;
}

/* Code block container backgrounds */
.bg-white\/60 {
  background-color: #fcfcfa !important;
}

.dark .bg-codeblock,
.dark .codeblock-dark {
  background-color: #1d1b16 !important;
}

#pagination {
  display: none;
}

/* Dark mode: use consistent black background for code sections */
.dark {
  --bg-color: #1d1b16;
}

/* Ensure the code example container has consistent dark background */
.dark .example-panel,
.dark [class*="example-panel"] {
  background-color: transparent !important;
}

/* Remove all button backgrounds in dark mode - outline only */
.dark .example-button {
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Active button gets orange background */
.dark .example-button.text-white {
  background-color: #fb631b !important;
}

/* Remove backgrounds from code block containers and cards in dark mode - outline only */
.dark [class*="Card"],
.dark .mint-card,
.dark a[href] > div {
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Remove hover backgrounds on card titles and content */
.dark [class*="Card"]:hover,
.dark .mint-card:hover,
.dark a[href]:hover > div,
.dark a[href] > div:hover {
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Remove background from code section container */
.dark .rounded-md.border {
  background-color: transparent !important;
}

/* Remove any highlighting from card containers and titles in light mode too */
[class*="Card"],
.mint-card,
[class*="Card"] h1,
[class*="Card"] h2,
[class*="Card"] h3,
[class*="Card"] h4 {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Remove card container backgrounds completely */
[class*="Card"] > div,
.mint-card > div,
[role="group"] > a > div {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Remove highlight from card title elements */
[class*="Card"] [class*="title"],
.mint-card [class*="title"] {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
}

/* Style Resource card titles to match the top cards */
[class*="Card"] h3,
[class*="Card"] h4,
.mint-card h3,
.mint-card h4 {
  font-size: 1.125rem !important; /* text-lg */
  font-weight: 600 !important; /* font-semibold */
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem !important; /* mb-2 */
}

/* Aggressive removal of any highlighting on all elements - but NOT in code blocks */
*:not(pre):not(code):not([class*="language-"]):not(.token) {
  text-decoration-color: inherit !important;
}

/* Remove any search highlights or selection backgrounds that might appear - but NOT in code */
mark:not(pre mark):not(code mark),
::selection:not(pre ::selection):not(code ::selection) {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
}

/* Remove highlight from any inline code or special elements - but exclude code blocks */
*:not(pre):not(code):not([class*="language-"]):not(.token)::before,
*:not(pre):not(code):not([class*="language-"]):not(.token)::after {
  background: inherit !important;
}

/* Target Mintlify-specific card components */
[data-card], [data-card-title], .card, .card-title {
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

/* Remove any underline decorations that might look like highlights */
a[href] h1, a[href] h2, a[href] h3, a[href] h4 {
  text-decoration: none !important;
  background: none !important;
  background-color: transparent !important;
}

/* Increase main navigation tabs font size (Home, Documentation, API Reference, Changelog) */
header nav button,
header [role="navigation"] a,
header [role="navigation"] button,
[data-tabs] a,
[data-tabs] button,
nav[data-orientation="horizontal"] a,
nav[data-orientation="horizontal"] button {
  font-size: 1.25rem !important; /* 20px - equivalent to text-xl */
}

/* Fix list bullets - scope to article content only, exclude navigation */
article ul:not(nav ul):not([role="navigation"] ul),
.prose ul:not(nav ul):not([role="navigation"] ul) {
  list-style-type: disc;
  list-style-position: outside;
  padding-left: 1.5rem; /* Adjust bullet indent */
}

article ol:not(nav ol):not([role="navigation"] ol),
.prose ol:not(nav ol):not([role="navigation"] ol) {
  list-style-type: decimal;
  list-style-position: outside;
  padding-left: 1.5rem;
}

article li:not(nav li):not([role="navigation"] li),
.prose li:not(nav li):not([role="navigation"] li) {
  display: list-item;
  margin-left: 0.5rem; /* Fine-tune bullet spacing */
}

/* Explicitly hide bullets in navigation/sidebar */
nav ul,
nav ol,
nav li,
[role="navigation"] ul,
[role="navigation"] ol,
[role="navigation"] li,
aside ul,
aside ol,
aside li {
  list-style-type: none !important;
  display: block;
}

/* Home page: cap prompt panel height and scroll inside */
.example-panel pre {
  max-height: 340px;
  overflow-y: auto;
  overflow-x: hidden;
  white-space: pre-wrap;
  word-break: break-word;
  scrollbar-gutter: stable;
}

/* Home page: hide Mintlify's built-in code block toolbar inside example panels.
   The toolbar is two sibling divs at top-right of each `.code-block`, both with
   a `print:hidden` class: one holds the Copy + Ask-AI buttons, the other is a
   white fade gradient that paints behind them. Hiding only the buttons left the
   white gradient showing as a "mystery white square" next to the scrollbar.
   We use our own Copy-prompt button instead. */
.example-panel .code-block > .print\:hidden {
  display: none !important;
}

/* Home page: scrollbar flush to the right edge of the prompt panel */
.example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar {
  width: 8px;
}
.example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.22);
  border-radius: 4px;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  background-clip: padding-box;
}
.dark .example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22);
  background-clip: padding-box;
}
.example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
  background: transparent;
}

/* Home page: vertical tab styling for the prompts picker (Figma spec —
   gray active state instead of orange tint). */
.example-tab-active {
  background-color: #eeeeee !important;
}
.dark .example-tab-active {
  background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06) !important;
}

/* Reset Mintlify's `.prose ul/li` styles for our tab list. The global
   prose rules add padding-left: 1.5rem to ul, margin-left: 0.5rem to li,
   and `display: list-item` (which can add internal padding-left). All of
   that pushes the tab buttons ~64px right of the column edge. */
.example-tab-list,
.example-tab-list li {
  padding: 0 !important;
  margin: 0 !important;
  list-style: none !important;
}
.example-tab-list li { display: block !important; }

/* Drop the prose-injected mt-5/mb-8 on Mintlify code blocks inside our
   prompt panels so the code block fits flush inside the gray surround
   with just the wrapper's p-2 visible as inset. */
.example-panel .code-block {
  margin-top: 0 !important;
  margin-bottom: 0 !important;
}

/* Home page: panel doc links */
.home-panel-link {
  font-size: 0.875rem;
  color: #4b5563;
  transition: color 0.15s;
}

.home-panel-link:hover {
  color: #FB631B;
}

.dark .home-panel-link {
  color: #9ca3af;
}

.dark .home-panel-link:hover {
  color: #FB631B;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Home page: backfill arbitrary Tailwind classes that Mintlify's static CSS
   tree-shakes (only ones used on this page). Without these, the home renders
   for ~2.8s with the wrong dimensions (full-width tab column, stacked MCP
   tiles, no padding) and snaps into place once runtime Tailwind hydrates —
   visible as a "pop-in" of the tab list and the MCP tile row.
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

/* Min-heights reserve layout space so first paint matches final paint. */
.min-h-\[260px\] { min-height: 260px; }

/* Custom colors used in the prompts picker + MCP tiles + panel header bar. */
.bg-\[\#f6f6f6\] { background-color: #f6f6f6; }
.bg-\[\#434343\] { background-color: #434343; }
.hover\:bg-\[\#2a2a2a\]:hover { background-color: #2a2a2a; }
.border-\[\#e5e5e5\] { border-color: #e5e5e5; }
.dark .dark\:bg-\[\#1a1a1a\] { background-color: #1a1a1a; }
.dark .dark\:border-\[\#2a2a2a\] { border-color: #2a2a2a; }
.dark .dark\:bg-\[\#111\] { background-color: #111; }

/* Tile + button micro-interactions. */
.bg-gray-50\/60 { background-color: rgb(249 250 251 / 0.6); }
.dark .dark\:bg-white\/\[0\.02\] { background-color: rgb(255 255 255 / 0.02); }

/* Text sizes used on MCP tiles + panel header. */
.text-\[12px\] { font-size: 12px; }
.text-\[13px\] { font-size: 13px; }

/* Responsive (lg+) layout for the prompts picker + MCP grid. Without these
   the tab column fills the full width and the MCP tiles stack 2-up instead
   of 4-up on desktop. */
@media (min-width: 1024px) {
  .lg\:w-\[262px\] { width: 262px; }
  .lg\:p-5 { padding: 1.25rem; }
  .lg\:grid-cols-4 { grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr)); }
  .lg\:border-r { border-right-width: 1px; }
  .lg\:border-b-0 { border-bottom-width: 0; }
  .lg\:border-gray-200 { border-color: rgb(229 231 235); }
  .dark .lg\:dark\:border-\[\#2a2a2a\] { border-color: #2a2a2a; }
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Overview page only: lift the *inner* content cap added by `mode: "wide"`.
   The outer `max-w-8xl` cap (1472px, also used by every other docs page) is
   kept so the sidebar stays in its normal position and the article column
   sits in the same total band as the rest of the docs. The inner cap added
   by wide-mode (`peer-[.is-wide]:[&>div:last-child]:max-w-6xl`, 1152px) is
   what was squishing the content into the leftmost ~1100px and leaving a
   big empty area where the right TOC normally lives. Removing only that
   one lets the page fill the full sidebar-to-TOC band without breaking the
   rest of the layout. Scoped via `data-current-path` on <html>.
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] [class*="max-w-6xl"] > div:last-child {
  max-width: none !important;
}

/* Overview page only: hide Mintlify's auto-generated page-header block
   (eyebrow + h1 title + description + Copy-page menu). The page is built
   as a custom landing experience that starts directly with the
   "Install the Parallel Search MCP" section, so the auto title is
   redundant. The whole block lives inside <header id="header">. */
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] header#header {
  display: none !important;
}

/* Overview page only: section headings ("Install the Parallel Search MCP",
   "Build with the Parallel APIs") should match the docs h1 weight. The
   global rule above forces all h*-tags to weight 500; override that just
   for headings that opt-in via the `.font-bold` Tailwind class on this
   page so they read at h1 weight without affecting any other page. */
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] h2.font-bold {
  font-weight: 700 !important;
}

/* Overview page only: zero out the prose-injected top margins that were
   designed to space content below the (now hidden) page header. Without
   these resets, the first heading sits ~90px below the top nav purely
   from stacked margins (h2 margin-top:60px from prose + mdx-content
   margin-top:32px from `mt-8`). The wrapper divs in the page already
   handle inter-section spacing, so we don't need any of this. */
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .mdx-content {
  margin-top: 0 !important;
}
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .mdx-content h2 {
  margin-top: 0 !important;
}

/* Overview page only: onboarding prompt body should match the
   Build-with-Parallel-APIs prompt panels — FT System Mono at the same
   13px Shiki size used inside .example-panel pre, normal weight. */
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt code {
  font-family: 'FT System Mono', monospace !important;
  font-size: 14px !important;
  font-weight: 400 !important;
  line-height: 1.6 !important;
}

/* Onboarding prompt link: match the shade of blue used for markdown
   `#` headings in the prompt panels below (rgb(5, 80, 174)). Tailwind
   arbitrary values like text-[#2563eb] get tree-shaken from Mintlify's
   static CSS bundle, so set color + weight here so they actually land. */
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a,
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:hover,
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:focus,
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:active,
html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:visited {
  color: rgb(5, 80, 174) !important;
  font-weight: 400 !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
  border-bottom: 0 !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}

.dark html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a,
.dark html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:hover,
.dark html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:focus,
.dark html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:active,
.dark html[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:visited,
html.dark[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a,
html.dark[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:hover,
html.dark[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:focus,
html.dark[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:active,
html.dark[data-current-path="/getting-started/overview"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a:visited {
  color: #79c0ff !important;
}

class="mint-sr-only" aria-hidden="false"><span data-as="p">For AI agents: a documentation index is available at <a href="https://docs.parallel.ai/llms.txt" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">https://docs.parallel.ai/llms.txt</a>. The full text of all docs is at <a href="https://docs.parallel.ai/llms-full.txt" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">https://docs.parallel.ai/llms-full.txt</a>. You may also fetch any page as Markdown by appending <code>.md</code> to its URL or sending <code>Accept: text/markdown</code>.</span></div>
<span data-as="p">The Parallel Search MCP Server provides drop-in web search and content extraction capabilities for any MCP-aware model. The tools invoke the <a class="link" href="/search/search-quickstart">Search API</a> and <a class="link" href="/extract/extract-quickstart">Extract API</a> endpoints but present a simpler interface to ensure effective use by agents. Search runs in <a class="link" href="/search/modes"><code>basic</code> mode</a> — tuned for low-latency responses inside agent loops — and total excerpts per tool call are capped to roughly 25,000 characters to stay within typical MCP client output limits.</span>

dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:!text-current [&amp;_kbd]:!text-current [&amp;_a]:!text-current [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:!text-current text-green-800 dark:text-green-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p"><strong>The Search MCP is free to use</strong> — no API key required, great for exploration and light use. For production use cases or higher rate limits, <a href="https://platform.parallel.ai" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">create a Parallel account</a> and pass your API key as a Bearer token in the <code>Authorization</code> header.</span></div></div>
<span data-as="p">The Search MCP comprises two tools:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>web_search</code></strong> — General-purpose web search inside an agent’s reasoning loop. Use when the agent needs current information or diverse sources across multiple angles.</li>
<li><strong><code>web_fetch</code></strong> — Pulls token-efficient markdown from specific URLs. Use after <code>web_search</code> narrows down candidates, or when the agent already has a URL it needs to read in depth.</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="one-click-install"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#one-click-install" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">One-Click Install</span></h2>

font-normal text-base leading-6 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400" data-component-part="card-content"><span data-as="p">One-click install for Goose.</span></div></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">For Claude Code, Codex CLI, Claude Desktop, Windsurf, Zed, Gemini CLI, Warp, Kiro, and other clients, see the <a href="#installation" class="link">Installation</a> section below — those clients use a CLI command or a JSON config file rather than deep-link URLs.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="use-cases"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#use-cases" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Use Cases</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The Search MCP is suited for any application where real-world information is needed as part of an AI agent’s reasoning loop. Common use cases include:</span>
<ul>
<li>Real-time fact checking and verification during conversations</li>
<li>Gathering current information to answer user questions</li>
<li>Researching topics that require recent or live data</li>
<li>Retrieving content from specific URLs to analyze or summarize</li>
<li>Competitive intelligence and market research</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="installation"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#installation" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Installation</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The Search MCP can be installed in any MCP client. Two endpoints are available:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>https://search.parallel.ai/mcp</code></strong> — default. Free to use anonymously at lower rate limits. Pass a Parallel API key as a Bearer token (<code>Authorization: Bearer &lt;key&gt;</code>) to unlock higher limits. <strong>OAuth is not advertised on this endpoint</strong>, so clients that support OAuth sign-in (Claude Desktop, Claude.ai custom connectors, Codex, etc.) will not prompt you to log in here — use <code>/mcp-oauth</code> below if you prefer OAuth.</li>
<li><strong><code>https://search.parallel.ai/mcp-oauth</code></strong> — the OAuth-capable endpoint. Requires authentication: Bearer API key OR the OAuth flow. Anonymous requests return <code>401</code>. Use this when you want OAuth instead of managing a Bearer key, or when you need to guarantee every request is attributed to a Parallel account — for example, organization-wide deployments, Zero Data Retention (ZDR) setups, or any context where unauthenticated traffic is not acceptable.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Swap <code>/mcp</code> for <code>/mcp-oauth</code> in any of the config snippets below if you want OAuth or enforced authentication. The Search MCP can also be <a class="link" href="/integrations/mcp/programmatic-use">used programmatically</a> by providing your Parallel API key in the Authorization header as a Bearer token.</span>

<span data-as="p">This URL connects anonymously. If you’d rather sign in with OAuth (so Claude manages the token for you), use <code>https://search.parallel.ai/mcp-oauth</code> instead — it triggers Claude’s OAuth sign-in flow.</span>
<span data-as="p">If you are part of an organization, you may not have access to custom connectors. Contact your organization administrator for assistance.</span>

dark:text-green-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p"><strong>Org admins (Team / Enterprise plans):</strong> to route Claude’s web queries through the Parallel Search MCP instead of Claude’s built-in web search, disable the native web search tool at <a href="https://claude.ai/admin-settings/capabilities" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link"><strong>Admin settings → Capabilities</strong></a>. Once off at the workspace level, members cannot re-enable it individually. See the <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/10684626-enabling-and-using-web-search" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Claude web search docs</a>.</span><span data-as="p">For the org-wide deployment, also point the connector at the <a href="#installation" class="link"><code>/mcp-oauth</code> endpoint</a> (not <code>/mcp</code>) so every request requires a Parallel API key or OAuth — useful when you need every call attributed to the organization.</span></div></div>
<span data-as="p">If you are not an admin, go to Settings → Developer → Edit Config and add the following JSON:</span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">    &quot;mcp-remote&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">    &quot;https://search.parallel.ai/mcp&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  ]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div><div class="print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">No API key is required for the Search MCP. To unlock higher rate limits, grab a key from <a href="https://platform.parallel.ai" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Platform</a> and append <code>&quot;--header&quot;, &quot;authorization: Bearer YOUR-PARALLEL-API-KEY&quot;</code> to the <code>args</code> array.</span>
<span data-as="p">For more details, see the <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/11175166-getting-started-with-custom-connectors-using-remote-mcp" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Claude remote MCP documentation</a>.</span>
<hr/>

<span data-as="p">Each command writes the equivalent <code>[mcp_servers.parallel-search]</code> entry to <code>~/.codex/config.toml</code> — you can also hand-edit it if you prefer.</span>
<span data-as="p">Restart Codex after adding the server. For more details, see the <a href="https://developers.openai.com/codex/mcp/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Codex MCP documentation</a>.</span>

Run <code>/mcp</code> inside Pi to verify the server is detected. For more details, see the <a href="https://github.com/nicobailon/pi-mcp-adapter" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">pi-mcp-adapter README</a>.</span><span data-as="p">Alternatively, skip MCP entirely and use the <a class="link" href="/integrations/cli">Parallel CLI</a> as a Pi skill — that’s the same pattern as our <a class="link" href="/integrations/clawhub">ClawHub / OpenClaw</a> integration.</span></div></details><details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose No API key is required; for higher rate limits, add an optional <code>headers</code> map with <code>&quot;Authorization&quot;: &quot;Bearer YOUR-PARALLEL-API-KEY&quot;</code> (key from <a href="https://platform.parallel.ai" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">platform.parallel.ai</a>).</span><span data-as="p"><code>openclaw mcp set</code> only writes to config — it doesn’t connect to the server or reload running agents. Start a new OpenClaw agent session (or restart your current one) for the tools to show up. Verify the saved definition with <code>openclaw mcp list</code> / <code>openclaw mcp show parallel-search</code>. For the full MCP CLI reference, see the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/mcp" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">OpenClaw MCP docs</a>.</span><span data-as="p">You can also skip MCP <span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div><div class="print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div><span data-as="p">Add an <code>--header &quot;Authorization: Bearer YOUR-PARALLEL-API-KEY&quot;</code> argument if you’re hitting <code>/mcp-oauth</code> or want higher rate limits.</span><span data-as="p">Placement differs per client — see the <a href="https://zed.dev/docs/ai/mcp" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Zed MCP docs</a>, <a href="https://docs.warp.dev/university/how-warp-uses-warp/using-mcp-servers-with-warp" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Warp MCP docs</a>, or <a href="https://manual.raycast.com/ai/model-context-protocol" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Raycast MCP docs</a> for the exact file location and wrapper format.</span></div></details></div>
<span data-as="p">If your client isn’t listed, most MCP clients accept the <code>mcpServers</code> format shown in the Cursor section. For clients that use a different wrapper (e.g., VS Code’s <code>mcp.servers</code>, Windsurf’s <code>serverUrl</code>), check the client’s documentation for the correct field names.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="best-practices"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#best-practices" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Best practices</span></h2>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="filtering-by-date-or-domain"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#filtering-by-date-or-domain" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Filtering by date or domain</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">To filter search results by date or domain, include these constraints directly in your search query or objective rather than expecting separate parameters. For example:</span>
<ul>
<li>“Latest AI research papers from 2026”</li>
<li>“News about climate change from nytimes.com”</li>
<li>“Product announcements from apple.com in the last month”</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">If date or domain filtering is important for your task, prompt the agent explicitly to include these details when using the tool.</span>
<div class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 14 14" fill="currentColor" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" aria-label="Note"><path fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd" d="M7 1.3C10.14 1.3 12.7 3.86 12.7 7C12.7 10.14 10.14 12.7 7 12.7C5.48908 12.6974 4.0408 12.096 2.97241 11.0276C1.90403 9.9592 1.30264 8.51092 1.3 7C1.3 3.86 3.86 1.3 7 1.3ZM7 0C3.14 0 0 3.14 0 7C0 10.86 3.14 14 7 14C10.86 14 14 10.86 14 7C14 3.14 10.86 0 7 0ZM8 3H6V8H8V3ZM8 9H6V11H8V9Z"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:!text-current [&amp;_kbd]:!text-current [&amp;_a]:!text-current [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:!text-current text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">We have experimented with adding dedicated date and domain parameters to the Search MCP tools, but found they harm quality overall—LLMs tend to overuse them, which overconstrains search results.</span></div></div>

<details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden py-4 px-5 space-x-2 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-t-xl" aria-controls="connection-timeout-or-server-unavailable-errors accordion children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="connection-timeout-or-server-unavailable-errors" class="absolute -top-[8rem]"></div><div class="mr-0.5" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg class="h-3 w-3 transition bg-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-400 duration-75" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/solid/caret-right.svg);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center"></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p class="m-0 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">Connection timeout or &#x27;server unavailable&#x27; errors</p></div></summary><div id="connection-timeout-or-server-unavailable-errors accordion children" role="region" aria-labelledby="connection-timeout-or-server-unavailable-errors" class="mt-2 mb-4 mx-6 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">If you’re getting connection errors:</span><ol>
<li><strong>Check your network:</strong> Ensure you can reach <code>https://search.parallel.ai</code></li>
<li><strong>Verify API key:</strong> Make sure your key is valid at <a href="https://platform.parallel.ai" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">platform.parallel.ai</a></li>
<li><strong>Check balance:</strong> A 402 error means insufficient credits—add funds to your account</li>
<li><strong>Restart your IDE:</strong> Some clients cache MCP connections</li>
</ol></div></details>

<li><strong>Restart your IDE</strong> completely (not just reload)</li>
<li><strong>Check configuration syntax:</strong> Ensure valid JSON with no trailing commas</li>
<li><strong>Verify the server URL:</strong> Must be exactly <code>https://search.parallel.ai/mcp</code></li>
<li><strong>Check IDE logs:</strong> Look for MCP-related errors in your IDE’s output/debug panel</li>

Gerstner Programm - Primary Font */\n@font-face {\n  font-family: 'Gerstner Programm';\n  src: url('https://assets.p0web.com/Gerstner-ProgrammRegular.woff2') format('woff2');\n  font-weight: 400;\n  font-style: normal;\n  font-display: swap;\n}\n\n@font-face {\n  font-family: 'Gerstner Programm';\n  src: url('https://assets.p0web.com/Gerstner-ProgrammMedium.woff2') format('woff2');\n  font-weight: 500;\n  font-style: normal;\n  font-display: swap;\n}\n\n/* Apply font family globally with fallbacks */\nbody, html {\n  font-family: 'Gerstner Programm', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Oxygen, Ubuntu, Cantarell, sans-serif;\n}\n\n/* FT System Mono - Secondary Font (Code) */\n@font-face {\n  font-family: 'FT System Mono';\n  src: url('https://assets.p0web.com/FTSystemMono-Regular.woff2') format('woff2');\n  font-weight: 400;\n  font-style: normal;\n  font-display: swap;\n}\n\n@font-face {\n  font-family: 'FT System Mono';\n  src: url('https://assets.p0web.com/FTSystemMono-Medium.woff2') format('woff2');\n  font-weight: 500;\n  font-style: normal;\n  font-display: swap;\n}\n\n/* Apply FT System Mono to code elements */\ncode, pre, .code, [class*=\"language-\"], kbd, samp, tt {\n  font-family: 'FT System Mono', monospace !important;\n}\n\n/* Global heading font weights - make them less bold */\nh1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {\n  font-weight: 500 !important; /* medium weight instead of bold/semibold */\n}\n\n/* Remove highlighting/backgrounds from card titles and headings */\nh1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {\n  background: none !important;\n  background-color: transparent !important;\n  background-image: none !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove any mark/highlight elements from headings */\nh1 mark, h2 mark, h3 mark, h4 mark, h5 mark, h6 mark {\n  background: none !important;\n  background-color: transparent !important;\n  color: inherit !important;\n}\n\n/* For extra light headings where explicitly needed */\n.font-normal h1,\n.font-normal h2,\n.font-normal h3 {\n  font-weight: 400 !important;\n}\n\n/* Consistent border radius for code snippets and cards */\npre,\ncode,\n[id*=\"code-group\"],\n.not-prose,\n[class*=\"codeblock\"],\n[role=\"tabpanel\"],\n.mint-card,\n[class*=\"Card\"],\n.rounded-xl,\n.rounded-lg,\n.rounded-2xl {\n  border-radius: 0.375rem !important; /* 5px equivalent to rounded-md */\n}\n\n/* Specific targeting for Mintlify code blocks */\n.flex.flex-col.not-prose.relative.overflow-hidden {\n  border-radius: 0.375rem !important;\n}\n\n/* Override blue colors with Parallel orange */\n/* Active tab in code blocks */\n[role=\"tab\"][aria-selected=\"true\"],\n[role=\"tab\"][data-headlessui-state=\"selected\"] {\n  color: #FB631B !important;\n  border-color: #FB631B !important;\n}\n\n/* Tab border bottom */\n[role=\"tab\"][aria-selected=\"true\"] .absolute,\n[role=\"tab\"][data-headlessui-state=\"selected\"] .absolute {\n  border-color: #FB631B !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove any blue backgrounds in dark mode */\n.dark .codeblock-dark,\n.dark [class*=\"codeblock\"],\n.dark pre {\n  background-color: #1d1b16 !important;\n}\n\n/* Ensure code block backgrounds match theme */\n[class*=\"codeblock\"],\n.not-prose {\n  background-color: #fcfcfa !important;\n}\n\n.dark [class*=\"codeblock\"],\n.dark .not-prose {\n  background-color: #1d1b16 !important;\n}\n\n/* Code block container backgrounds */\n.bg-white\\/60 {\n  background-color: #fcfcfa !important;\n}\n\n.dark .bg-codeblock,\n.dark .codeblock-dark {\n  background-color: #1d1b16 !important;\n}\n\n#pagination {\n  display: none;\n}\n\n/* Dark mode: use consistent black background for code sections */\n.dark {\n  --bg-color: #1d1b16;\n}\n\n/* Ensure the code example container has consistent dark background */\n.dark .example-panel,\n.dark [class*=\"example-panel\"] {\n  background-color: transparent !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove all button backgrounds in dark mode - outline only */\n.dark .example-button {\n  background-color: transparent !important;\n  background-image: none !important;\n}\n\n/* Active button gets orange background */\n.dark .example-button.text-white {\n  background-color: #fb631b !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove backgrounds from code block containers and cards in dark mode - outline only */\n.dark [class*=\"Card\"],\n.dark .mint-card,\n.dark a[href] \u003e div {\n  background-color: transparent !important;\n  background-image: none !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove hover backgrounds on card titles and content */\n.dark [class*=\"Card\"]:hover,\n.dark .mint-card:hover,\n.dark a[href]:hover \u003e div,\n.dark a[href] \u003e div:hover {\n  background-color: transparent !important;\n  background-image: none !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove background from code section container */\n.dark .rounded-md.border {\n  background-color: transparent !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove any highlighting from card containers and titles in light mode too */\n[class*=\"Card\"],\n.mint-card,\n[class*=\"Card\"] h1,\n[class*=\"Card\"] h2,\n[class*=\"Card\"] h3,\n[class*=\"Card\"] h4 {\n  background: none !important;\n  background-color: transparent !important;\n  background-image: none !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove card container backgrounds completely */\n[class*=\"Card\"] \u003e div,\n.mint-card \u003e div,\n[role=\"group\"] \u003e a \u003e div {\n  background: none !important;\n  background-color: transparent !important;\n  background-image: none !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove highlight from card title elements */\n[class*=\"Card\"] [class*=\"title\"],\n.mint-card [class*=\"title\"] {\n  background: none !important;\n  background-color: transparent !important;\n}\n\n/* Style Resource card titles to match the top cards */\n[class*=\"Card\"] h3,\n[class*=\"Card\"] h4,\n.mint-card h3,\n.mint-card h4 {\n  font-size: 1.125rem !important; /* text-lg */\n  font-weight: 600 !important; /* font-semibold */\n  margin-bottom: 0.5rem !important; /* mb-2 */\n}\n\n/* Aggressive removal of any highlighting on all elements - but NOT in code blocks */\n*:not(pre):not(code):not([class*=\"language-\"]):not(.token) {\n  text-decoration-color: inherit !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove any search highlights or selection backgrounds that might appear - but NOT in code */\nmark:not(pre mark):not(code mark),\n::selection:not(pre ::selection):not(code ::selection) {\n  background: none !important;\n  background-color: transparent !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove highlight from any inline code or special elements - but exclude code blocks */\n*:not(pre):not(code):not([class*=\"language-\"]):not(.token)::before,\n*:not(pre):not(code):not([class*=\"language-\"]):not(.token)::after {\n  background: inherit !important;\n}\n\n/* Target Mintlify-specific card components */\n[data-card], [data-card-title], .card, .card-title {\n  background: none !important;\n  background-color: transparent !important;\n  background-image: none !important;\n}\n\n/* Remove any underline decorations that might look like highlights */\na[href] h1, a[href] h2, a[href] h3, a[href] h4 {\n  text-decoration: none !important;\n  background: none !important;\n  background-color: transparent !important;\n}\n\n/* Increase main navigation tabs font size (Home, Documentation, API Reference, Changelog) */\nheader nav button,\nheader [role=\"navigation\"] a,\nheader [role=\"navigation\"] button,\n[data-tabs] a,\n[data-tabs] button,\nnav[data-orientation=\"horizontal\"] a,\nnav[data-orientation=\"horizontal\"] button {\n  font-size: 1.25rem !important; /* 20px - equivalent to text-xl */\n}\n\n/* Fix list bullets - scope to article content only, exclude navigation */\narticle ul:not(nav ul):not([role=\"navigation\"] ul),\n.prose ul:not(nav ul):not([role=\"navigation\"] ul) {\n  list-style-type: disc;\n  list-style-position: outside;\n  padding-left: 1.5rem; /* Adjust bullet indent */\n}\n\narticle ol:not(nav ol):not([role=\"navigation\"] ol),\n.prose ol:not(nav ol):not([role=\"navigation\"] ol) {\n  list-style-type: decimal;\n  list-style-position: outside;\n  padding-left: 1.5rem;\n}\n\narticle li:not(nav li):not([role=\"navigation\"] li),\n.prose li:not(nav li):not([role=\"navigation\"] li) {\n  display: list-item;\n  margin-left: 0.5rem; /* Fine-tune bullet spacing */\n}\n\n/* Explicitly hide bullets in navigation/sidebar */\nnav ul,\nnav ol,\nnav li,\n[role=\"navigation\"] ul,\n[role=\"navigation\"] ol,\n[role=\"navigation\"] li,\naside ul,\naside ol,\naside li {\n  list-style-type: none !important;\n  display: block;\n}\n\n/* Home page: cap prompt panel height and scroll inside */\n.example-panel pre {\n  max-height: 340px;\n  overflow-y: auto;\n  overflow-x: hidden;\n  white-space: pre-wrap;\n  word-break: break-word;\n  scrollbar-gutter: stable;\n}\n\n/* Home page: hide Mintlify's built-in code block toolbar inside example panels.\n   The toolbar is two sibling divs at top-right of each `.code-block`, both with\n   a `print:hidden` class: one holds the Copy + Ask-AI buttons, the other is a\n   white fade gradient that paints behind them. Hiding only the buttons left the\n   white gradient showing as a \"mystery white square\" next to the scrollbar.\n   We use our own Copy-prompt button instead. */\n.example-panel .code-block \u003e .print\\:hidden {\n  display: none !important;\n}\n\n/* Home page: scrollbar flush to the right edge of the prompt panel */\n.example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar {\n  width: 8px;\n}\n.example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {\n  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.22);\n  border-radius: 4px;\n  border: 1px solid transparent;\n  background-clip: padding-box;\n}\n.dark .example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {\n  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22);\n  background-clip: padding-box;\n}\n.example-panel pre::-webkit-scrollbar-track {\n  background: transparent;\n}\n\n/* Home page: vertical tab styling for the prompts picker (Figma spec —\n   gray active state instead of orange tint). */\n.example-tab-active {\n  background-color: #eeeeee !important;\n}\n.dark .example-tab-active {\n  background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06) !important;\n}\n\n/* Reset Mintlify's `.prose ul/li` styles for our tab list. The global\n   prose rules add padding-left: 1.5rem to ul, margin-left: 0.5rem to li,\n   and `display: list-item` (which can add internal padding-left). All of\n   that pushes the tab buttons ~64px right of the column edge. */\n.example-tab-list,\n.example-tab-list li {\n  padding: 0 !important;\n  margin: 0 !important;\n  list-style: none !important;\n}\n.example-tab-list li { display: block !important; }\n\n/* Drop the prose-injected mt-5/mb-8 on Mintlify code blocks inside our\n   prompt panels so the code block fits flush inside the gray surround\n   with just the wrapper's p-2 visible as inset. */\n.example-panel .code-block {\n  margin-top: 0 !important;\n  margin-bottom: 0 !important;\n}\n\n/* Home page: panel doc links */\n.home-panel-link {\n  font-size: 0.875rem;\n  color: #4b5563;\n  transition: color 0.15s;\n}\n\n.home-panel-link:hover {\n  color: #FB631B;\n}\n\n.dark .home-panel-link {\n  color: #9ca3af;\n}\n\n.dark .home-panel-link:hover {\n  color: #FB631B;\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   Home page: backfill arbitrary Tailwind classes that Mintlify's static CSS\n   tree-shakes (only ones used on this page). Without these, the home renders\n   for ~2.8s with the wrong dimensions (full-width tab column, stacked MCP\n   tiles, no padding) and snaps into place once runtime Tailwind hydrates —\n   visible as a \"pop-in\" of the tab list and the MCP tile row.\n   --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/* Min-heights reserve layout space so first paint matches final paint. */\n.min-h-\\[260px\\] { min-height: 260px; }\n\n/* Custom colors used in the prompts picker + MCP tiles + panel header bar. */\n.bg-\\[\\#f6f6f6\\] { background-color: #f6f6f6; }\n.bg-\\[\\#434343\\] { background-color: #434343; }\n.hover\\:bg-\\[\\#2a2a2a\\]:hover { background-color: #2a2a2a; }\n.border-\\[\\#e5e5e5\\] { border-color: #e5e5e5; }\n.dark .dark\\:bg-\\[\\#1a1a1a\\] { background-color: #1a1a1a; }\n.dark .dark\\:border-\\[\\#2a2a2a\\] { border-color: #2a2a2a; }\n.dark .dark\\:bg-\\[\\#111\\] { background-color: #111; }\n\n/* Tile + button micro-interactions. */\n.bg-gray-50\\/60 { background-color: rgb(249 250 251 / 0.6); }\n.dark .dark\\:bg-white\\/\\[0\\.02\\] { background-color: rgb(255 255 255 / 0.02); }\n\n/* Text sizes used on MCP tiles + panel header. */\n.text-\\[12px\\] { font-size: 12px; }\n.text-\\[13px\\] { font-size: 13px; }\n\n/* Responsive (lg+) layout for the prompts picker + MCP grid. Without these\n   the tab column fills the full width and the MCP tiles stack 2-up instead\n   of 4-up on desktop. */\n@media (min-width: 1024px) {\n  .lg\\:w-\\[262px\\] { width: 262px; }\n  .lg\\:p-5 { padding: 1.25rem; }\n  .lg\\:grid-cols-4 { grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr)); }\n  .lg\\:border-r { border-right-width: 1px; }\n  .lg\\:border-b-0 { border-bottom-width: 0; }\n  .lg\\:border-gray-200 { border-color: rgb(229 231 235); }\n  .dark .lg\\:dark\\:border-\\[\\#2a2a2a\\] { border-color: #2a2a2a; }\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   Overview page only: lift the *inner* content cap added by `mode: \"wide\"`.\n   The outer `max-w-8xl` cap (1472px, also used by every other docs page) is\n   kept so the sidebar stays in its normal position and the article column\n   sits in the same total band as the rest of the docs. The inner cap added\n   by wide-mode (`peer-[.is-wide]:[\u0026\u003ediv:last-child]:max-w-6xl`, 1152px) is\n   what was squishing the content into the leftmost ~1100px and leaving a\n   big empty area where the right TOC normally lives. Removing only that\n   one lets the page fill the full sidebar-to-TOC band without breaking the\n   rest of the layout. Scoped via `data-current-path` on \u003chtml\u003e.\n   --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/getting-started/overview\"] [class*=\"max-w-6xl\"] \u003e div:last-child {\n  max-width: none !important;\n}\n\n/* Overview page only: hide Mintlify's auto-generated page-header block\n   (eyebrow + h1 title + description + Copy-page menu). The page is built\n   as a custom landing experience that starts directly with the\n   \"Install the Parallel Search MCP\" section, so the auto title is\n   redundant. The whole block lives inside \u003cheader id=\"header\"\u003e. */\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/getting-started/overview\"] header#header {\n  display: none !important;\n}\n\n/* Overview page only: section headings (\"Install the Parallel Search MCP\",\n   \"Build with the Parallel APIs\") should match the docs h1 weight. The\n   global rule above forces all h*-tags to weight 500; override that just\n   for headings that opt-in via the `.font-bold` Tailwind class on this\n   page so they read at h1 weight without affecting any other page. */\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/getting-started/overview\"] h2.font-bold {\n  font-weight: 700 !important;\n}\n\n/* Overview page only: zero out the prose-injected top margins that were\n   designed to space content below the (now hidden) page header. Without\n   these resets, the first heading sits ~90px below the top nav purely\n   from stacked margins (h2 margin-top:60px from prose + mdx-content\n   margin-top:32px from `mt-8`). The wrapper divs in the page already\n   handle inter-section spacing, so we don't need any of this. */\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/getting-started/overview\"] .mdx-content {\n  margin-top: 0 !important;\n}\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/getting-started/overview\"] .mdx-content h2 {\n  margin-top: 0 !important;\n}\n\n/* Overview page only: onboarding prompt body should match the\n   Build-with-Parallel-APIs prompt panels — FT System Mono at the same\n   13px Shiki size used inside .example-panel pre, normal weight. */\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/getting-started/overview\"] .agent-onboarding-prompt code {\n  font-family: 'FT System Mono', monospace !important;\n  font-size: 14px !important;\n  font-weight: 400 !important;\n  line-height: 1.6 !important;\n}\n\n/* Onboarding prompt link: match the shade of blue used for markdown\n   `#` headings in the prompt panels below (rgb(5, 80, 174)). Tailwind\n   arbitrary values like text-[#2563eb] get tree-shaken from Mintlify's\n   static CSS bundle, so set color + weight here so they actually land. */\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/getting-started/overview\"] .agent-onboarding-prompt a,\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/getting-started/overview\"] .agent-onboarding-prompt
